Result: 随机化初始化使单量子比特基准的保真度达到0.929，未知系统的保真度达到0.787；迹距离分别降至0.124和0.316。啁啾激励主要通过加速收敛和降低轨迹密度损失来改善优化。成功将学习到的哈密顿量映射到色散区间的双量子比特总线谐振腔架构，获得包括transmon电容、Josephson电感、量子比特间距和总线谐振腔长度在内的关键电路参数。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了一条从黑盒量子系统识别到物理量子仿真的数据驱动路径，在量子数字孪生建模方面具有潜在应用价值，为未知量子系统的识别与仿真提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: Accurate identification of unknown quantum systems is essential for quantum computing, sensing, and control because the Hamiltonian governs quantum state evolution. This work proposes a QNN based framework for black box Hamiltonian learning and quantum system emulation using full density matrix trajectory learning. Unlike approaches based only on final states or selected observables, the method exploits the complete temporal evolution of the density matrix under Lindblad dynamics. A synthetic dataset of physically admissible Hamiltonians and dissipation parameters is generated to emulate experimental measurements. The QNN learns a nonlinear mapping from control inputs to a 32-dimensional Hamiltonian coefficient vector, enabling reconstruction and differentiable emulation of the unknown system. Chirped excitation and randomized initial quantum states are incorporated to improve robustness and provide richer dynamical information. Performance is evaluated using trajectory density loss, quantum-state fidelity, and trace distance. Randomized initialization improves state-level reconstruction, increasing fidelity to 0.929 for the single qubit benchmark and 0.787 for the unknown system, while reducing trace distance to 0.124 and 0.316, respectively. In contrast, chirped excitation primarily improves optimization by accelerating convergence and reducing trajectory density loss. Finally, the learned Hamiltonian is mapped onto a physical two qubit bus resonator architecture in the dispersive regime, yielding key circuit parameters including transmon capacitances, Josephson inductances, qubit separation, and bus-resonator length. The framework therefore establishes a data-driven pathway from black box quantum system identification to physical quantum emulation, with potential applications in quantum digital twin modeling.

</details>


### [98] [What do position and time mean in the quantum wavefunction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23032)
*Mustafa Bakr,Zichi Zhang,Margot Stakenborg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的量子力学教学框架，围绕波函数ψ(x,t)中隐藏的两个映射（时间演化选择轨迹上的态，谱表示将该态映射到可观测量的本征值标签），厘清位置表示、广义本征矢、时间算符等概念在数学角色与记号之间的区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生在学习量子力学时容易过度解读ψ(x,t)记号，产生x和t是否具有相同数学地位、ψ(t)是否需要广义bra等困惑。这些问题跨越课程不同部分，缺乏统一处理框架。

Method: 构建两个映射的教学框架：(1)时间演化沿Hilbert空间轨迹选择态；(2)谱表示将态映射到可观测结果标签。不使用广义本征矢重构位置表示，以有限格点极限恢复Dirac的|x>记法和delta归一化条件。区分背景坐标、平移参数、可观测量、谱标签和物理记录等概念，并借助自旋、电路QED和光钟实验实例进行验证。

Result: 澄清了Stone定理类比，区分了预定时间位置测量与到达时间测量，阐明Pauli论证的强形式所排除的内容，并给出一个存在正则自伴随时间算符的可精确求解边界案例。

Conclusion: 目的并非提出时间的新诠释，而是提供一个可复用的教学框架，帮助学习者区分数学角色与记号，避免对ψ(x,t)的过度解读。

Abstract: The notation $ψ(x,t)$ is among the first pieces of quantum mechanics that students learn. It is also among the easiest to over-interpret. Because $x$ and $t$ occur as arguments of the same function, students may ask whether they have the same mathematical status. They may also ask whether $ψ(t)$ should require a generalized bra $\bra{t}$ in the same way that $ψ(x)=\braket{x}ψ$ is often written. A related question is whether the absence of a universal time operator follows simply from Pauli's argument. These questions mix several structures that are usually introduced in different parts of the curriculum. We present a unified pedagogical treatment built around two maps hidden in $ψ(x,t)$. Time evolution selects a state along a trajectory in Hilbert space. A spectral representation then maps that state to amplitudes labelled by outcomes of a chosen observable. We formulate the position representation without generalized eigenkets. We recover Dirac's $\ket{x}$ notation as a controlled continuum shorthand and use a finite-grid limit to show where delta normalization enters. We distinguish background coordinates, translation parameters, observables, spectral labels, and physical records. We also clarify the Stone-theorem analogy, compare prescribed-time position measurements with arrival-time measurements, state what the strong form of Pauli's argument excludes, and exhibit an exactly solvable boundary case in which a canonical self-adjoint time observable exists. Spin, circuit-QED, and optical-clock examples provide experimentally grounded checks. The aim is not a new interpretation of time. It is a reusable teaching framework for separating mathematical role from notation.

</details>


### [99] [Partial-Moment PINNs for Caldeira--Leggett Parameter Learning in Quantum Brownian Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23093)
*Krishna Bhatia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究从部分矩迹中恢复Caldeira-Leggett（量子布朗）振子参数，提出一种矩级PINN，通过自动微分强制线性CL/HPZ常微分方程，并利用PSD（Cholesky）协方差头、高温CL假设及涨落-耗散关系施加物理约束。在合成数据上，该约束变体可准确恢复(ω,γ)并稳定D_pp，优于有限差分和Kalman-EM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子布朗振子的参数恢复是不良条件问题，尤其是从部分可观测的矩数据（如缺少σ_pp或D_pp）中恢复摩擦系数γ和扩散系数D_pp时存在病态性。传统方法（有限差分、Kalman-EM）在部分观测下精度不足或条件数差，需要一种能内嵌物理定律并利用涨落-耗散关系的可微方法来解决参数可识别性问题。

Method: 采用矩级物理信息神经网络（PINN）：(1) 网络预测前五个一阶/二阶矩，通过自动微分强制满足线性CL/HPZ ODE；(2) 使用PSD协方差头（Cholesky分解）保证协方差半正定性；(3) 施加高温CL假设，令D_xp≈0；(4) 利用涨落-耗散关系将D_pp与γ绑定；(5) 仅利用部分通道{μ_x, σ_xx, σ_xp}拟合，并通过Fisher信息风格的可辨识性检验评估哪些矩观测必须存在（至少需要一个方差观测量），稀疏σ_pp作为“锚点”可恢复条件数。

Result: 在合成CL数据上，约束PINN变体准确恢复角频率ω和阻尼系数γ，稳定扩散系数D_pp，并取得比有限差分和带精确Van Loan离散化的Kalman-EM更低的rollout误差。Fisher风格检验表明扩散参数辨识需至少一个方差观测量，稀疏σ_pp锚点可改善条件数。同一PINN框架还能学习时变HPZ系数。

Conclusion: 矩级PINN结合物理结构约束（PSD协方差、高温假设、涨落-耗散关系）从部分矩迹中恢复量子布朗振子参数是可行且稳健的方法，且该方法具备泛化到时变HPZ系统的能力，为量子扩散系统参数辨识提供了新的可微概率编程范式。

Abstract: We study parameter recovery in the Caldeira--Leggett (quantum Brownian) oscillator from partial moment traces. Our model is a moment-level PINN that predicts the five first/second moments and enforces the linear CL/HPZ ODEs by automatic differentiation. Physical structure is imposed through a PSD (Cholesky) covariance head, high-temperature CL assumptions with $D_{xp}\approx0$, and fluctuation--dissipation ties between $D_{pp}$ and $γ$. On synthetic CL data with channels ${μ_x,σ_{xx},σ_{xp}}$, the constrained variant recovers $(ω,γ)$ accurately, stabilizes $D_{pp}$, and achieves low rollout error compared to finite differences and Kalman--EM (expectation--maximization) with exact Van Loan discretization. Fisher-style checks confirm that diffusion needs at least one variance observable, and sparse $σ_{pp}$ ``anchors'' restore conditioning. We also show that the same PINN can learn time-varying HPZ coefficients.

</details>


### [100] [Physics-Guided Linear Mapper for Quantum Error Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23109)
*Tulsi Chaudhari,Krishna Bhatia,Shalini Devendrababu,Srinjoy Ganguly,Luis Gerardo Ayala Bertel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理引导的线性映射器（PGLM），利用电路复杂度和设备校准数据中的七个可解释特征进行量子误差缓解，在52个模拟基准电路上实现了显著误差预测改进（3量子位电路RMSE降低50.1%），并具备亚毫秒级推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子误差缓解方法多依赖黑盒机器学习，缺乏可解释性、数据效率低且延迟高，难以满足含噪中等规模量子（NISQ）设备上变分算法对实时性和可解释性的需求。

Method: 提出PGLM（物理引导线性映射器），从电路复杂度和设备校准数据中提取七个可解释特征，构建线性映射模型用于量子误差预测与缓解；同时设计电路规模感知的部署策略，根据电路大小选择是否启用PGLM。

Result: 在52个模拟基准电路（1-4量子位）上，3量子位电路RMSE降低50.1%，4量子位电路降低32.3%；单量子位电路性能下降。电路规模感知部署策略实现32.6%的总体改善。亚毫秒推理时间支持变分算法集成。系数分析表明电路深度和CNOT数量主导误差预测。

Conclusion: PGLM作为数据高效、可解释、低延迟的量子误差缓解方案展现出潜力，尤其适用于噪声累积明显的多量子位场景；结果基于理想化噪声模型的模拟，还需硬件验证作为未来工作。

Abstract: We introduce a novel physics-guided linear mapper (PGLM) for quantum error mitigation that uses seven distinct interpretable features derived from circuit complexity and device calibration data. The goal is to provide a data-efficient, interpretable, and low-latency alternative to the black-box machine learning for quantum error mitigation in noisy-intermediate scale quantum devices. Evaluated on 52 simulated benchmark circuits (1--4 qubits), PGLM demonstrates strong performance in noise-accumulation regimes: 50.1% RMSE reduction on 3-qubit circuits and 32.3% on 4-qubit circuits, while single-qubit circuits show degraded performance. A circuit-size-aware deployment policy achieves 32.6% aggregate improvement. Sub-millisecond inference enables integration into variational algorithms, and analysis of learned coefficients reveals that circuit depth and CNOT count dominate error prediction, consistent with decoherence mechanisms. Results are simulator-based with idealized noise models; hardware validation remains essential future work.

</details>


### [101] [Dark-Mode Control of Contrasting Entanglement and Bell Nonlocality between Mechanical Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23113)
*Souvik Agasti,Philippe Djorwe,Xin Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由两个机械振子耦合到公共腔的光机系统，用于生成纯的双模压缩机械稳态。研究发现Bell非定域性与纠缠最大化不在同一位置出现，且光机耦合不完美性可增强机械纠缠但抑制Bell非定域性。通过引入相位相关的声子跳跃，可在特定暗模配置中选择性增强Bell非定域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续变量多模机械系统中，如何同时实现高纠缠和高Bell非定域性（量子非局域相关性）是一个关键挑战。需要理解纠缠与Bell非定域性之间的关系，并找到克服两者此消彼长矛盾的方法。

Method: 提出了两个机械振子耦合到公共腔的光机械系统模型；系统分析光机耦合不完美性对纠缠和非定域性的影响；引入相位相关的声子跳跃（phase-dependent phonon hopping）来调控暗模配置；通过分析Bogoliubov模布居的不平衡性来解释状态纯度变化。

Result: （1）Bell非定域性的违反并不出现在纠缠最大的位置；（2）光机耦合缺陷可增强机械纠缠但降低状态纯度从而抑制Bell非定域性；（3）引入相位相关声子跳跃后，可在暗模配置中选择性增强Bell非定域性，即使总体纠缠下降；（4）该架构与现有微波腔光机平台兼容。

Conclusion: 该方案为多模机械系统中的非局域量子关联控制提供了实验可行的途径，可作为连续变量隐形传态和高保真量子通信的有前景平台。

Abstract: This study presents a detailed proposal for an optomechanical system consisting of two mechanical oscillators coupled to a common cavity, aimed at generating pure and entangled two-mode squeezed mechanical steady states. We found that the violation of Bell's measurement may not occur where the entanglement is maximum; rather, nonlocality can be observed for lower entangled states. A central result is that optomechanical coupling imperfections can enhance mechanical entanglement while simultaneously suppressing Bell nonlocality by reducing the purity of the mechanical state. To mitigate this trade-off, we introduce phase-dependent phonon hopping between the mechanical oscillators and show that Bell nonlocality can be selectively enhanced in specific dark-mode configurations, even when the overall entanglement is reduced. We trace this contrasting behaviorto changes in state purity associated with the imbalance of the Bogoliubov-mode occupations. Compatible with existing microwave cavity optomechanical platforms, the proposed architecture provides an experimentally accessible route for controlling nonlocal quantum correlations in multimode mechanical systems. Our proposed scheme serves as an attractive platform for the deployment of continuous-variable teleportation and high-fidelity quantum communication.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum Reservoir Computing with Physics-Informed Correction for Reduced-Order PDE Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23119)
*Krishna Bhatia,Harsh,Shalini Devendrababu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种混合"提议-校正"架构用于降阶PDE预测：量子储备池计算（QRC）预测潜变量系数动力学，基于PINN的物理信息校正器（PIC）细化局部滚动窗口。在Burgers和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky（KS）方程上评估，结果显示在KS（主要混沌基准）上QRC+PIC在RMSE、NRMSE和PDE残差上均优于单独QRC；而Burgers则揭示了简单基线仍具竞争力的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将量子储备池计算（QRC）与物理信息校正机制相结合，构建基准相关的降阶PDE预测策略，以提升混沌系统（如KS方程）的预测精度。

Method: 采用混合提议-校正架构：QRC作为纯态量子储备池负责预测潜变量系数动力学（proposal），PINN-based物理信息校正器（PIC）对局部滚动窗口进行细化修正（correction）。在Burgers方程和KS方程两个基准上进行评估，其中KS作为主要混沌基准。

Result: 在KS方程上，QRC+PIC在RMSE、NRMSE和PDE残差三个指标上均一致优于单独QRC；Burgers方程则显示简单基线仍表现强劲的场景，说明该方法具有基准依赖性。

Conclusion: QRC提议配合局部物理信息校正是可行的、基准依赖的降阶预测策略，在混沌场景（KS）中优势明显，但在较简单场景（Burgers）中提升有限。

Abstract: We study a hybrid proposal--correction architecture for reduced-order PDE forecasting in which a pure-state quantum reservoir computer (QRC) predicts latent coefficient dynamics and a PINN-based physics-informed corrector (PIC) refines local rollout windows. The method is evaluated on Burgers and Kuramoto--Sivashinsky (KS), with KS as the primary chaotic benchmark. On KS, QRC+PIC consistently improves over QRC alone in RMSE, NRMSE, and PDE residual, while Burgers highlights a regime in which simple baselines remain strong. These results suggest that QRC proposals with local physics-informed correction are a viable benchmark-dependent reduced-order forecasting strategy.

</details>


### [103] [Efficient Quantum Simulation of Linearized Vlasov--Poisson Dynamics Using Trotter and THRIFT Hamiltonian Simulation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23127)
*Kartick Paul,Rahul V,S. Aravinda,Reetesh K. Gangwar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用量子仿真框架求解线性化Vlasov-Poisson方程，通过对离散系统进行Hermitian哈密顿量重构，实现了磁化与非磁化等离子体的门控量子计算模拟。系统比较了不同阶Trotter-Suzuki乘积公式与THRIFT方法的性能，并引入Richardson外推作为误差缓解技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vlasov-Poisson系统是等离子体动力学的基本描述，其相空间的高维特性使得经典数值模拟计算成本极高。需要探索量子计算方法来提高等离子体动力学的仿真效率。

Method: 将线性化Vlasov-Poisson方程的离散系统重构为适合门控量子计算的Hermitian哈密顿量；使用一阶、二阶、四阶Trotter-Suzuki乘积公式和THRIFT方法实现时间演化；通过电场演化、态保真度、收敛行为、能量守恒、纠缠熵及量子资源需求（电路深度和双比特门复杂度）系统评估各方法；结合Richardson外推降低有限时间步误差。

Result: 对磁化和非磁化等离子体模型进行了全面的方法比较，揭示了Trotter与THRIFT方法的性能差异，并验证了Richardson外推在不增加电路深度的前提下有效降低时间步长误差。

Conclusion: 为门控量子计算机上精确且资源高效的等离子体动力学量子模拟提供了实际指导准则，建立了Trotter与THRIFT方法的综合比较框架。

Abstract: The Vlasov--Poisson system provides the fundamental kinetic description of plasma and plays a central role in understanding collective phenomena such as Landau damping and wave--particle interactions. Efficient numerical simulation of these dynamics remains challenging because of the high dimensionality of phase space. In this work, we studied magnetized and non-magnetized plasma using a quantum simulation framework for the linearized Vlasov-Poisson equation by reformulating the discretized system as a Hermitian Hamiltonian suitable for gate-based quantum computation. The time evolution is implemented using first, second and fourth-order Trotter--Suzuki product formulas and the recently proposed Time-Resolved Interaction Framework (THRIFT). The performance of the different simulation methods is systematically evaluated through electric field evolution, state fidelity, convergence behavior, energy conservation, entanglement entropy and quantum resource requirements, including circuit depth and two-qubit gate complexity, for both magnetized and non-magnetized plasma models. To further reduce finite time step errors without increasing circuit depth, Richardson extrapolation is incorporated as a error-mitigation technique. The results provide a comprehensive comparison of Trotter and THRIFT approaches and establish practical guidelines for accurate and resource-efficient quantum simulation of plasma dynamics on gate-based quantum computers.

</details>


### [104] [Taming Spacetime Overhead and Design Complexity in Distributed Fault-Tolerant Superconducting Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23159)
*Qinjing Yu,Ke Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于表面码的模块化分布式超导量子计算架构，通过硬件锚定的协同设计和资源估算协议，证明相对于单片式架构，分布式架构仅需适度额外资源开销，且开销近乎规模不变，从而将芯片尺寸从架构参数转变为可由可制造性和控制封装约束决定的工程自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错超导量子计算机的扩展需要分布式架构，但核心疑问在于有噪声且缓慢的片间操作是否会带来显著的时空开销或协调负担，这一问题尚无系统性、硬件接地气的回答。

Method: 提出硬件接地的架构协同设计，将片间延迟和噪声限制在模块边界以避免全局瓶颈；同时建立综合硬件约束和电路级纠错性能的资源估算协议，以RSA-2048因子分解为基准，在实验锚定参数下进行资源估算。

Result: 与理想单片式基线相比，分布式架构在量子比特数和执行时间上仅需适度额外开销，且开销在广泛的模块容量窗口内近乎规模不变，实现了芯片尺寸与全局性能的解耦。

Conclusion: 分布式容错超导量子计算是可行的，其扩展不会伴随资源增长或协调负担的爆炸式增加；模块容量从需要精细调节的架构参数转变为灵活的工程自由度。

Abstract: Scaling fault-tolerant superconducting quantum computers will likely require distributed architectures built from multiple manufacturable quantum processing units. A central question is whether noisy and slow inter-chip operations impose substantial spacetime overhead or orchestration burdens compared with monolithic architectures. To answer this question, we present a hardware-grounded architectural co-design together with a comprehensive resource-estimation protocol for surface-code-based modular processors. The design confines inter-chip latency and noise to module boundaries, preventing slow, noisy links from inducing prohibitive spacetime overhead or becoming a global orchestration bottleneck. The resource-estimation protocol integrates hardware constraints and circuit-level error-correction performance into utility-scale algorithmic cost estimates, enabling a controlled assessment of different architectures. Using RSA-2048 factorization as a demanding benchmark, we estimate resources under experimentally anchored parameters and realistic superconducting hardware constraints. Compared with a large, ideal monolithic baseline, the resulting distributed architecture requires only modest additional resource overhead in both qubit count and execution time. More importantly, the overhead is nearly scale-invariant across a broad module-capacity window, decoupling chip size from global performance. This decoupling turns module capacity from a finely tuned architectural parameter into a flexible engineering degree of freedom, allowing chip sizes to be set by manufacturability and control-packaging constraints rather than architectural fine-tuning. These results establish a viable route to distributed fault-tolerant superconducting quantum computation that scales without prohibitive resource growth or heavy orchestration burden.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum observables as Fréchet sensitivity kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23191)
*Rafael Abreu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子力学的变分-伴随解释，将正向波函数与伴随波函数的相互作用定义为系统的广义灵敏度核（Fréchet相互作用密度）。Born概率密度是该框架的特例（当伴随波函数取正向波函数的复共轭时），由此表明概率是一种特殊形式（正定）的灵敏度，而非独立的公理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子力学将Born概率规则视为一个基本假设，但对其"为何概率恰好等于波函数模平方"缺乏更深层的解释。作者希望通过变分-伴随框架，将概率降级为更一般灵敏度理论的特殊情形，从而统一理解各类量子观测量（动量、能量、自旋等）的灵敏度核。

Method: 采用变分-伴随（variational-adjoint）方法，引入伴随波函数与正向波函数相互作用形成Fréchet相互作用密度，作为系统对参数或波函数扰动的灵敏度核。通过选取伴随波函数为正向波函数的复共轭这一特殊选择，证明Born概率密度成为该框架的特例。该方法还扩展到其他量子观测量，生成对应的Fréchet灵敏度核。

Result: 证明了Born概率密度是Fréchet相互作用密度在伴随波函数等于正向波函数复共轭时的特殊情形，此时相互作用密度为实且非负。此外，该方法推广到动量、能量、自旋等其他量子观测量，表明存在一类更广泛的Fréchet灵敏度核。该框架还建立了与时间对称量子力学解释之间的联系。

Conclusion: 变分-伴随解释将概率重新定义为灵敏度的一种特殊正定形式，暗示Born规则可能不是量子力学的基本公设，而是一般变分灵敏度理论的必然结果。该框架为量子控制、量子计量学提供了潜在的新工具，并与时间对称的量子力学解释相互关联。

Abstract: We present a variational--adjoint interpretation of quantum mechanics in which the interaction between forward and adjoint wavefunctions defines a general sensitivity kernel of the system. This Fréchet interaction density quantifies how perturbations in the wavefunction (or system parameters) influence a chosen observable. The familiar Born probability density appears as a special case when the adjoint wavefunction is chosen as the complex conjugate of the forward wavefunction, for which the interaction density becomes real and non-negative. Within this framework, probability is a particular positive-definite form of sensitivity described by the Fréchet interaction density.
  In general, the variational--adjoint interpretation also produces Fréchet sensitivity kernels associated with other quantum observables, including momentum, energy, and spin. This suggests that the Born probability density belongs to a broader class of Fréchet sensitivity kernels associated with quantum observables. The proposed interpretation also provides a connection with time-symmetric interpretations of quantum mechanics and possible future applications in quantum control and quantum metrology.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum Monte Carlo in the Age of Many-Body Quantum Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23231)
*Yi-Ming Ding,Bin-Bin Mao,Zheng Yan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法在量子多体信息诊断中的最新进展，涵盖纠缠熵、纠缠谱、Rényi负性、稳定子熵以及退相干驱动现象等多个非线性可观测量，并聚焦于自旋-1/2（qubit）系统，同时推广至qudit和玻色子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息为多体物理提供了全新的信息论视角和诊断工具，但传统的QMC方法仅限于测量线性可观测量。为了从量子信息角度深入理解强关联量子多体系统，需要将QMC方法扩展至非线性量子信息量的测量。

Method: 文章从统一视角出发，系统梳理了将QMC适配于量子多体信息领域的最新进展，涵盖了多种非线性诊断量的提取方法，包括纠缠熵与纠缠谱（Rényi、von Neumann等）、用于混合态纠缠的Rényi负性、用于量子魔术（magic）的稳定子熵，以及退相干驱动现象中虚时演化与退相干相互作用及强-弱自发对称性破缺等主题。

Result: 该综述为将QMC方法推广到量子信息相关非线性可观测量提供了统一的框架和方法论总结，展示了这些方法在纠缠、魔术资源、混合态纠缠及开放系统退相干现象中的可行性与应用前景。

Conclusion: QMC已成为研究量子多体信息论诊断的核心数值工具，其适用性已从传统线性可观测量拓展至广泛的非线性量子信息量，为强关联多体系统与量子信息科学的交叉研究提供了重要方法学支撑。

Abstract: Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) methods are among the central numerical tools for studying strongly correlated quantum many-body systems, particularly in higher dimensions. As quantum information has introduced new information-theoretic perspectives and diagnostics into many-body physics, QMC methods have accordingly been extended beyond the measurement of conventional linear observables. This review summarizes recent progress in adapting QMC to many-body quantum-information, focusing on qubit or spin-$1/2$ systems as a concrete setting while keeping the discussion broadly applicable to qudit and bosonic systems. We present a unified perspective on the extraction of nonlinear diagnostics, including entanglement entropies and entanglement spectra, Rényi negativities for mixed-state entanglement, stabilizer entropies for quantum magic, and decoherence-driven phenomena such as the interplay between imaginary-time evolution and decoherence and strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [107] [Non-Hermitian Generalization of Bloch Sphere in Spacetime Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23232)
*Chih-Wei Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用时空代数（STA）框架，将非厄米哈密顿量驱动的两能级量子系统的态空间从单位2-球面推广到未来光锥，并证明非酉时间演化对应洛伦兹变换，将动力学分类为空间旋转（PT对称）、纯boost（反PT对称）、零旋转（奇异点）和一般混合四类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Bloch球几何只适用于厄米量子系统，而实际物理系统中广泛存在非厄米哈密顿量（如开放系统、增益/损耗系统）。需要一个统一的几何框架来理解非厄米量子系统的态空间结构和动力学分类，特别是PT对称量子力学中的奇异点拓扑性质。

Method: 作者采用时空代数（Spacetime Algebra, STA）这一几何代数工具，将态密度算符从STA的偶子代数提升到全代数。在这种表述下，态空间从单位2-球面（对应厄米系统的纯态集合）扩张为未来光锥（null cone），而一般非厄米哈密顿量驱动的非酉时间演化恰好对应于洛伦兹群中的正常时正变换（proper orthochronous Lorentz transformations）。通过这一几何对应关系，系统地将哈密顿量动力学分类为四种几何类别。

Result: 成功建立了非厄米两能级系统的完整几何图像：光谱成了未来光锥上的null向量；四类动力学分别对应空间旋转（PT对称系统）、纯boost（反PT对称系统）、零旋转（奇异点/exceptional points）和一般混合变换。该框架还为PT对称量子力学的若干已知结果（包括奇异点的拓扑特征）提供了新的几何解释。

Conclusion: 本文为理解非厄米量子系统提供了优雅而统一的几何语言。通过STA实现的光锥-洛伦兹变换对应关系，不仅推广了Bloch球的经典几何图像，还为PT对称量子力学中的奇异点拓扑提供了自然的几何诠释，开辟了利用洛伦兹几何研究非厄米量子系统的途径。

Abstract: We establish a geometric generalization of the Bloch sphere for two-level quantum systems with non-Hermitian Hamiltonians using the Spacetime Algebra (STA) formulation. By lifting the state density operator from the even subalgebra to the full STA, we show that the state space expands from the unit 2-sphere to a future light cone. The non-unitary time evolution generated by a general non-Hermitian Hamiltonian corresponds to proper orthochronous Lorentz transformations on the null vectors. We classify the Hamiltonian dynamics into four distinct geometric classes: spatial rotations (corresponding to $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric systems), pure boosts (anti-$\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric systems), null rotations (exceptional points), and general mixtures. We also use this formulation to study several results from PT-symmetric quantum mechanics, including the topological features of the exceptional points.

</details>


### [108] [Efficient Computation of QKD Key Rates without Semidefinite Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23285)
*Bence Temesi,Antoine Gansel,Gereon Koßmann,Rene Schwonnek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅需特征值计算即可估算量子密钥分发（QKD）安全密钥率的计算方法，将内存需求从d^4降至d^2，并在树莓派等低资源设备上实现了实时密钥率估算，性能远超现有工作站基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD设备运行中需要将观测数据转化为可靠的安全密钥率估计，但现有方法依赖半定规划或熵锥规划，其内存需求随希尔伯特空间维度d按d^4规模增长，资源消耗巨大，难以嵌入硬件进行实时分析。

Method: 提出一种仅依赖特征值计算的数值方法用于安全密钥率估计，将内存需求从d^4降低到d^2。算法用不到100行Common Lisp代码实现，并可通过有理近似以极低开销引入非数值验证（如严格证明）。

Result: 在配备1GB内存和Cortex-A53处理器的树莓派上实现了实时密钥率估算，尽管硬件资源有限，性能仍比现有基于工作站的基准快多个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法为将完整的数值安全分析直接嵌入QKD硬件铺平了道路，使QKD设备能够自主、实时地进行安全密钥率估计。

Abstract: Translating observed data into a reliable estimate of the secure key rate is a crucial step for operating a quantum key distribution device. We provide a computational method for this task that only requires eigenvalue computations and is therefore both fast and resource efficient. In contrast, existing approaches rely on semidefinite programming or programming on the entropy cone, whose memory requirements can scale as $d^4$ in the underlying Hilbert-space dimension. Our method reduces this requirement to $d^2$. A minimal implementation of our algorithm takes fewer than 100 lines of Common Lisp. We demonstrate real-time key-rate estimation on a Raspberry Pi with a 1 GB memory and a Cortex-A53 processor. Despite these modest resources, our implementation outperforms existing workstation-based benchmarks by several orders of magnitude. Non-numerical verification can be incorporated with little overhead using rational approximations. These results open the way toward embedding complete numerical security analysis directly into qkd hardware.

</details>


### [109] [Exact Quasiprobability Hierarchy of the Double-Morse Oscillator: From Potential Geometry to Operator Ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23316)
*F. Chogle,B. Teklu,M. F. Pereira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对对称双Morse振子的最低准精确基态进行了表示一致性分析，区分了参数A（改变物理势能几何）与Cahill-Glauber排序参数s（仅改变表示方式）的作用。文中给出了Wigner函数和Weyl特征函数的闭式表达式，证明该态的Wigner函数在A趋近1时负区域缩小并非经典化，而是物理几何变化与表示选择的分离效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相空间与准概率方法在量子技术中至关重要，用于表征局域化、非高斯性、非经典资源和粗粒化。然而，物理参数（A）与表示参数（s）对相空间结构的影响常常被混淆。需要一个精确基准来分离这两种作用，并检验非线性相空间方法的有效性。

Method: 作者对对称双Morse振子的最低准精确基态进行了分析，推导出Wigner函数和Weyl特征函数的闭式表达式。利用Weyl函数生成对称有序的矩和累积量，从而构造完整的s-有序层次。通过分析不同s取值下的相空间表示（Wigner、Husimi、P-表示），系统比较了物理参数A与表示参数s各自的影响。

Result: Wigner函数展示了依赖于A的位置与动量局域化之间的交换，并保留负值区域，从而证明该纯态的非经典性和非高斯性。对于s>0的Husimi表示，各向同性高斯平滑抑制了精细符号变化结构但保留了大规模局域化包络，其非负性不隐含经典性。P表示仍为分布性的，不构成正则的正相干态混合。随着A增大，Wigner负区域表观缩小并非经典化。

Conclusion: A控制物理几何，而s控制同一非高斯、非经典结构在不同表示中的呈现方式。这种分离为非线性相空间方法提供了一个精确基准。

Abstract: Phase-space and quasiprobability methods play key roles across quantum technologies, characterizing localization, non-Gaussianity, nonclassical resources, and coarse-graining. We present a representation-consistent analysis of the lowest quasi-exact ground state of the symmetric double-Morse oscillator. The parameter $A$ changes the potential geometry and physical state, whereas the Cahill--Glauber ordering parameter $s$ changes only the representation and phase-space resolution of a fixed density operator. Although the potential is double-welled for $0<A<1$, the exact ground-state amplitude is single-peaked at the origin and lies above the internal barrier; as $A$ approaches unity, the merged well remains locally quartic rather than harmonic. We obtain closed expressions for the Wigner function and Weyl characteristic function. The Wigner function shows the $A$-dependent exchange between position and momentum localization and retains negative regions, certifying nonclassicality and, for this pure state, non-Gaussianity. The Weyl function gives the Fourier-dual description, generates symmetrically ordered moments and cumulants, and yields the full $s$-ordered hierarchy. For $s<0$, isotropic Gaussian smoothing suppresses fine sign-changing structure while preserving the large-scale localization envelope. The Husimi endpoint is nonnegative without implying classicality, whereas the Glauber--Sudarshan $P$ representation remains distributional and does not define a regular positive coherent-state mixture. Thus, the reduced visual extent of Wigner-negative regions as $A$ increases is not classicalization: $A$ controls the physical geometry, while $s$ controls how the same non-Gaussian, nonclassical structure appears across complementary representations. This separation provides an exact benchmark for nonlinear phase-space methods. theory.

</details>


### [110] [Entanglement-enhanced fluctuation-free daemonic ergotropy with random measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23372)
*Saptarshi Roy,Pritam Halder,Tamal Guha,Hyunseok Jeong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机化测量下的daemonic ergotropy（恶魔功），发现随机化测量会打破量子-经典态与纠缠纯态在工作提取上的等价性，并建立了随机增益与测量诱导涨落之间的权衡关系。纠缠纯态可实现无涨落的代数最大增益，而量子-经典态的任何正增益必然伴随涨落，因此随机化恶魔功可作为纠缠认证的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的优化daemonic ergotropy研究表明，通过辅助测量做功提取时，量子-经典态可以在固定系统边际下复现纠缠态的最大功，使得纠缠在热力学意义上可被替代。但该等价性是否在测量随机化条件下依然成立尚不清楚，这关系到纠缠是否构成量子热力学优势的本质资源。

Method: 针对qudit-qubit量子-经典态，在Haar随机投影测量下推导平均daemonic增益的上界，并证明增益-涨落权衡关系。对两量子比特纠缠纯态，计算其代数最大增益并证明其对任意测量基均无涨落。进一步将结论推广到一般两量子比特可分态，并考察从最优基周围极冠采样的部分随机化测量场景。

Result: 证明量子-经典态在随机化测量下的正平均增益必然伴随测量诱导涨落；而一类两量子比特纠缠纯态在任意测量基下均保持无涨落且达到系统边际允许的代数最大增益，至少是同等边际量子-经典态增益的两倍。该结论对一般可分态亦成立，随机化增益成为纠缠认证的充分判据。部分随机化（极冠采样）下纠缠优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 随机化daemonic ergotropy将测量诱导的功涨落与关联类型（量子纠缠 vs 经典关联）联系起来，确立了随机化恶魔功作为纠缠的热力学探针，展示了熵不确定性原理与量子热力学的深层关联。

Abstract: Optimized daemonic ergotropy can make entanglement thermodynamically dispensable in measurement-assisted work extraction: for a fixed system marginal, quantum-classical states can reproduce the maximal work obtainable by optimizing the auxiliary measurement. We show that this equivalence is broken when the auxiliary measurement is randomized. For qudit-qubit quantum-classical states under Haar-random projective measurements, we derive upper bounds on the averaged daemonic gain and prove a gain-fluctuation trade-off, showing that any positive randomized gain necessarily entails measurement-induced fluctuations. In sharp contrast, a family of two-qubit entangled pure states attains the algebraic maximum of the gain allowed by a system marginal while remaining fluctuation-free for every measurement basis, yielding at least twice the gain achievable by any quantum-classical state with the same marginal. We further demonstrate that such conclusion holds when general two-qubit separable states are considered positioning randomized gain as a sufficient criterion for entanglement certification. Finally, we show that the entanglement advantage persists under partially randomized measurements sampled from a polar cap around the optimal basis. These results establish randomized daemonic ergotropy as a thermodynamic probe of entanglement and exhibit the connection between measurement-induced work fluctuations on the type of correlations: quantum entanglement vs classical.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum-Geometric Length Scale for Long-distance Squeezing in Bosonic Bogoliubov Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23380)
*Sonu Verma,Sangmo Cheon,Myung-Joong Hwang,Moon Jip Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明参量配对可在玻色子晶格中产生完全平坦的Bogoliubov能带，由紧致Bogoliubov生成元张成。这些生成元编码了量子几何结构，其规范Bogoliubov模的挤压变换随动量变化，由挤压扇区辛量子度量量化。复动量奇点同时决定反常关联衰减长度和度量的动量空间宽度，构成内在量子几何长度尺度，可连续调谐以控制关联和挤压的空间范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模挤压是连续变量量子技术的核心资源，但其空间范围通常受色散传播限制。作者希望突破这一限制，探索如何在平坦能带系统中产生空间延伸的量子资源，从而在不依赖色散传播的情况下实现长程关联和挤压。

Method: 通过参量配对构造完全平坦的Bogoliubov能带，使用紧致Bogoliubov生成元（混合产生和湮灭算符）编码挤压结构。由于相邻平移生成元存在非零对易重叠，需通过空间扩展的线性组合构建满足规范对易关系的Bogoliubov模。引入挤压扇区辛量子度量量化挤压变换，并通过解析延拓到复动量空间分析奇异性与衰减长度的关联。

Result: 复动量奇点同时设定了反常关联的衰减长度和辛量子度量的动量宽度，定义了一个内在量子几何长度尺度。该奇点可在保持能带完全平坦的同时连续调谐，从而控制关联和挤压的空间范围。偏离精确平坦性时，长程关联通过多个衰减通道持续存在；缺陷局域模和二聚化边界模可探测底层量子几何；弱阻尼极限下可通过频率滤波的双端口关联提取该量子几何长度尺度。

Conclusion: 规范Bogoliubov模的量子几何是实现空间扩展量子资源（源自紧致Bogoliubov生成元）的普遍机制。这一发现为在平坦能带系统中设计和控制长程压缩与关联资源提供了理论基础和实验可观测的探针。

Abstract: Multimode squeezing is a key resource for continuous-variable quantum technologies, but its spatial range in bosonic lattices is usually tied to dispersive propagation. Here we show that parametric pairing can create an exactly flat Bogoliubov band spanned by compact Bogoliubov generators, while producing phase-sensitive anomalous correlations and sub-vacuum collective-mode squeezing that extend far beyond their finite support. Each compact generator mixes annihilation and creation operators, thereby encoding the Bogoliubov squeezing structure, and neighboring translated generators can have nonzero commutator overlap. Enforcing canonical bosonic commutation relations therefore requires spatially extended linear combinations of these translated compact generators, which define the canonical Bogoliubov modes. The squeezing transformation of these modes varies with momentum and is quantified by the squeezing-sector symplectic quantum metric. A complex-momentum singularity of the analytically continued canonical Bogoliubov modes sets both the anomalous-correlation decay length and the momentum-space width of this metric, defining an intrinsic quantum-geometric length scale. This singularity can be tuned continuously while preserving exact flatness, thereby controlling the spatial range of correlations and squeezing. Away from exact flatness, long-distance correlations persist through multiple decay channels, while modes localized by defects and dimerized boundaries provide complementary probes of the underlying quantum geometry. In the weak-damping limit, the same quantum-geometric length scale can be extracted from frequency-filtered two-port correlations. Our results establish the quantum geometry of canonical Bogoliubov modes as a mechanism for spatially extended quantum resources arising from compact Bogoliubov generators.

</details>


### [112] [Dual-Isotope Sympathetic Cooling in a Long Ion Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23386)
*Tianyi Wang,Marko Cetina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文演示了在室温下对23离子链（包含qubit和coolant离子）进行稳态中电路同情冷却，可抑制所有与计算相关的运动模式的加热噪声，同时保持qubit相干性和纠缠门操作的基态，并将冷却方案扩展到并行qubit重置协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维离子链因其高可控性和连接性而成为近期量子计算机的有吸引力的构建模块，但长离子链易受外部波动电场引起的运动加热影响。为支持深度计算，该噪声必须在不可引起qubit退相干或降低qubit连接性的前提下被抑制。

Method: 在23离子链中采用同情冷却（sympathetic cooling）方案，对长波长轴向和径向模式保持其态制备后的平均声子占据数，同时对用于纠缠门实施的模式在整个56ms电路中保持接近基态；冷却序列不使用任何qubit操作。此外还利用物种间共享的径向模式耦合实现了并行的qubit重置协议。

Result: 冷却方案在室温系统中成功将qubit和coolant离子混合链的所有计算相关运动模式维持在低声子占据状态，保持qubit相干性不受影响，并评估了对单比特和两比特门操作的影响，同时实现了基于共享径向模式耦合的并行qubit重置。

Conclusion: 该同情冷却方案是减少单独寻址的长离子链中门错误的关键步骤，并建立了一个用于模拟开放量子系统的多功能平台，为近期的离子阱量子计算提供了重要的噪声抑制手段。

Abstract: Owing to their high controllability and connectivity, one-dimensional ion chains are attractive building blocks for near-term quantum computers. However, long ion chains are susceptible to motional heating caused by fluctuating external electric fields. To enable deep computations, this source of noise must be suppressed without inducing qubit decoherence or degrading qubit connectivity. We demonstrate steady-state mid-circuit sympathetic cooling of all computationally-relevant motional modes of a 23-ion chain consisting of \qubit qubit and \coolant coolant ions without using any qubit operations. In a room-temperature system, our cooling scheme preserves the mean phonon occupations of the long-wavelength axial and radial modes near their values following state preparation, while keeping the modes used to implement entangling gates near their ground states throughout a 56-ms circuit. Crucially, we show that our cooling sequence preserves qubit coherence, and evaluate its impact on single- and two-qubit gate operations. Additionally, we demonstrate a parallel qubit reset protocol leveraging the shared radial mode coupling between species. Our sympathetic cooling scheme represents a critical step toward reducing gate errors in individually-addressed long ion chains and establishes a versatile platform for simulating open quantum systems.

</details>


### [113] [Exact Fock-State Preparation with $n^{1/4}$ Circuit Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23389)
*Tanay Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性单参数（D1p）协议，将无限维希尔伯特空间中的大数福克态制备映射为二维振幅放大问题，仅需O(n^{1/4})次迭代即可实现高保真度制备，制备|10^6⟩态仅需39次迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制备高效、确定且高保真度的大数福克态是扩展玻色子量子技术和探索高激发能量下量子现象的关键，而现有方法在可扩展性和保真度方面存在局限。

Method: 提出D1p协议，从振幅|α|≃√n的相干态出发，将福克态制备映射到二维振幅放大问题；利用位移操作和数选择性相位操作实现相位匹配，无需数值优化；并扩展至态转移、一般叠加态、有限维系统和多体纠缠态，同时提出多目标形式制备L腿猫态。

Result: 初始目标态布居数按n^{-1/2}标度，迭代数和电路深度为O(n^{1/4})，理想模型下保真度为单位值，制备|10^6⟩仅需39次迭代；多目标形式制备L腿猫态，最多十腿的猫态仅需两次迭代，与相干态振幅无关。

Conclusion: 该框架为在支持基本控制操作的平台上制备高激发玻色子态提供了广泛适用的途径。

Abstract: Efficient, deterministic, and high-fidelity preparation of large Fock states is essential for scaling bosonic quantum technologies and exploring quantum phenomena at large excitation energies. We introduce a deterministic one-parameter (D1p) protocol that maps Fock-state preparation in an infinite-dimensional Hilbert space onto two-dimensional amplitude amplification. Starting from a coherent state with $|α|\simeq\sqrt{n}$, the initial target-state population scales as $n^{-1/2}$, yielding an iteration count and circuit depth of $\mathcal{O}(n^{1/4})$. Phase matching guarantees unit fidelity in the ideal model; remarkably, preparing $|{10^6}\rangle$ requires only 39 iterations. The protocol uses only displacements and number-selective phase operations, requires no numerical optimization, and further extends to state transfer, general superpositions, finite-dimensional systems, and multipartite entangled states. In the large-amplitude regime, its multi-target form prepares $L$-legged cat states with an iteration count determined only by $L$; cats with up to ten legs require only two iterations, independent of the coherent-state amplitude. This framework provides a broadly applicable route to highly excited bosonic states on platforms supporting these elementary controls.

</details>


### [114] [Quantifying the dimensionality of multiparticle entanglement via partition rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23399)
*Sophia Denker,Ismaël Septembre,Robin Krebs,Otfried Gühne*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于纯态分解为无真多粒子纠缠态叠加的纠缠维度概念，用于表征多粒子系统的纠缠维度，并提供了构造性方法来刻画纯态和混合态的划分秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠作为量子技术资源的价值随系统规模增大（更多粒子或更高维度）而提升，但维度与多粒子纠缠之间的关联尚不清晰。目前仅对两粒子系统有基于Schmidt分解的明确纠缠维度概念，多粒子系统缺乏类似的统一框架。

Method: 引入基于纯态分解为无真多粒子纠缠态叠加的纠缠维度概念；提供构造性方法表征纯态和混合态的划分秩；将该框架与张量的切片秩和划分秩相关联，并连接到广义注入张量范数。

Result: 该方法能够识别新的最大纠缠相关态，并实现对随机局域操作与经典通信下量子态的分类。从数学角度，该方法将纠缠维度问题转化为张量的切片秩和划分秩的表征。

Conclusion: 该工作为多粒子纠缠维度的系统化研究提供了新框架，连接了量子信息理论与张量代数，有助于深入理解纠缠维度与多粒子纠缠的相互作用。

Abstract: The usefulness of entanglement as a resource in quantum technologies increases for larger systems, that is, if more particles or higher-dimensional quantum systems are considered. Yet, the interplay between dimensionality and multiparticle entanglement is not well understood. Only for two-particle systems an unambiguous and coherent notion of entanglement dimensionality, based on the Schmidt decomposition, is known. We introduce a concept to characterize the entanglement dimensionality of multiparticle states based on decompositions of pure states into superpositions of states without genuine multiparticle entanglement. We provide constructive methods to characterize the resulting partition rank for pure and mixed states. This allows the identification of novel maximally correlated states as well as a discrete classification of quantum states under stochastic local operations and classical communication. From a mathematical perspective, our approach can be formulated in terms of the slice rank and partition rank of tensors and our results allow to characterize these by connecting them to a generalized injective tensor norm.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum-enhanced sensing in a driven-dissipative system via chiral waveguide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23433)
*Yan Xi Foo,Saubhik Sarkar,Leong Chuan Kwek,Abolfazl Bayat,Davit Aghamalyan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一类由相干驱动二能级系统与手性波导耦合的耗散驱动系统，证明了其稳态对弱失谐强度展现出增强的灵敏度，且探测制备时间仅随系统规模线性增长，从而在计入制备时间后仍能维持量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子传感器虽可达到经典传感器无法企及的精度，但环境耗散会严重损害其性能。因此，探索如何利用耗散而非对抗耗散，研究耗散驱动系统的传感性能以获取实际量子优势显得至关重要。

Method: 研究一个由相干驱动（失谐频率）的二能级系统阵列耦合到手性波导的体系，其中波导既作为相互作用媒介又作为热库。通过解析方法分析该系统的稳态性质，建立测量协议以实现增强灵敏度，并利用波导手性调控灵敏度的精度与范围。

Result: 解析结果表明，该系统的稳态对弱失谐强度展现出增强的灵敏度，且探测制备时间仅随系统尺寸线性增长，因此增强灵敏度在计入制备时间后依然成立。同时建立了实验上可实现的测量协议。

Conclusion: 手性波导的引入使得耗散得以被利用为传感优势，实现了在耗散驱动系统中获得可持续的量子增强灵敏度，并可通过波导手性灵活调控灵敏度的精度与增强范围。

Abstract: Quantum sensors can achieve precision unattainable by classical sensors. However, dissipation coming from interaction with the environment can greatly degrade the performance of quantum sensors. Therefore, it is crucial to attempt harnessing dissipation to our advantage and investigate the sensing performance of driven-dissipative systems to gain practical quantum advantages. In this paper, we study a system of two-level systems driven coherently at a detuned frequency, and importantly, connected to a chiral waveguide that serves both as an interaction mediator and a bath. We show analytically that the steady state of such a system shows enhanced sensitivity to estimate weak detuning strength while the probe preparation time only grows linearly with system size. Therefore, the enhanced sensitivity is sustained even when the preparation time is incorporated. We further establish measurement protocols to achieve such sensitivity that are experimentally implementable. The chirality of the waveguide helps us to control both precision and range of enhanced sensitivity.

</details>


### [116] [Satisfying Quantum Codes: Physics-Informed and Hardware-Aware Code Design with SAT Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23460)
*Ben DalFavero,William M. Watkins,Margarite L. LaBorde,Vincent Russo,Ethan Egger,Gregory Quiroz,Ryan LaRose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个高度通用且灵活的量子纠错码计算设计框架，将码设计形式化为布尔可满足性（SAT）问题，可同时考虑物理启发和硬件感知约束。作者证明了码设计问题是NP完全的，但实验表明最先进的SAT求解器仍能高效解决许多实际问题，包括在数分钟内设计含100个物理量子比特的物理启发码，以及为偏置噪声设计出逻辑错误率低于最先进表面码的新型硬件感知码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码的设计目前主要依靠人工完成，没有充分考虑具体问题或硬件约束。这限制了量子纠错在实际应用中的效能，因此需要一种自动化的、可同时融入物理启发性与硬件感知性的码设计方法。

Method: 将量子码设计问题转化为布尔可满足性（SAT）问题，将所需的纠错准则全部编码为SAT约束，利用最先进的SAT求解器进行自动搜索设计；同时从计算复杂性角度证明了该问题的NP完全性。

Result: 理论层面证明了量子码设计是NP完全问题；实践层面，SAT求解器能够在数分钟内设计出包含多达100个物理量子比特的物理启发码，并为偏置噪声设计了逻辑错误率低于最先进表面码的新型硬件感知码。

Conclusion: 尽管量子码设计在一般情况下是NP完全的，但借助现代SAT求解器，仍可在实际规模上自动设计出性能优异的量子纠错码，为量子纠错码的自动化设计提供了一条可行且有效的路径。

Abstract: Although quantum error correction is widely believed to be necessary for impactful applications of quantum computers, the design of quantum error correction codes is largely done by hand, without respect to problem or hardware constraints. In this work, we present a highly general and flexible framework for the computational design of both physics-inspired and hardware-aware quantum codes. To do so, we formulate code design as a Boolean satisfiability (SAT) problem and show how to incorporate all required error correction criteria. We prove that code design is NP-complete, ruling out any efficient algorithm for designing codes in general. Nonetheless, we show that state-of-the-art SAT solvers are able to effectively find solutions for many practical problems. Notably, we are able to design physics-inspired codes with up to 100 physical qubits in minutes, and we design new hardware-aware codes for biased noise which have a lower logical error rate than state-of-the-art surface codes.

</details>


### [117] [Dissipation-induced Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev physics in many-body cavity quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23557)
*Pietro Pacchioni,Filippo Ferrari,Vincenzo Savona,Matteo Seclì*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出腔量子电动力学（QED）装置可以实现耗散的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）物理。超冷费米子在无序、全对全腔介导相互作用下提供两条互补路径：单模腔中的原子自发辐射和多模腔中的光子泄漏。两条路径尽管分别源于可积和混沌的闭系统极限，但都收敛到相同的非厄米随机矩阵普适类。单模情形中，耗散从可积哈密顿量中产生了量子混沌。作者将这种收敛归因于耗散秩的可调增长（分别由Lamb-Dicke参数和腔模间距控制），并通过单原子分辨密度中从长寿命预热记忆到快速热化的交叉这一动力学指纹予以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索开放多体系统中量子混沌的普适实现方法，证明腔QED装置可作为耗散SYK物理的平台，并揭示由可积系统通过耗散生成量子混沌的机制。

Method: 利用腔QED系统中的超冷费米子，通过单模腔原子自发辐射和多模腔光子泄漏两条实验路径实现无序、全对全腔介导相互作用，分析两者的非厄米随机矩阵普适性，并研究耗散秩（由Lamb-Dicke参数和腔模间距控制）的可调增长对混沌生成的影响。

Result: 两条路径均收敛到相同的非厄米随机矩阵普适类；单模腔情形下耗散可从可积哈密顿量生成量子混沌；耗散秩的可调增长（分别受Lamb-Dicke参数和腔模间距控制）是这一收敛的关键；混沌表现为从长寿命预热记忆到快速热化的动力学交叉，可见于单原子分辨密度。

Conclusion: 腔QED是实现耗散SYK物理的可行平台，耗散秩的增长提供了从可积性到量子混沌的可调控制机制，为开放量子系统中的量子混沌研究提供了新的实验途径。

Abstract: We show that cavity quantum electrodynamics (QED) devices can realize dissipative Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) physics, a paradigmatic setting for quantum chaos in open many-body systems. Ultracold fermions with disordered, all-to-all cavity-mediated interactions provide two complementary routes: atomic spontaneous emission in a single-mode cavity and photon leakage from a multimode cavity. Strikingly, both converge to the same non-Hermitian random-matrix universality despite originating from integrable and chaotic closed-system limits, respectively. In the single-mode case, dissipation therefore creates quantum chaos from an integrable Hamiltonian. We trace this convergence to a tunable growth in dissipative rank, controlled, respectively, by the Lamb-Dicke parameter and the cavity-mode spacing. The resulting chaos leaves a dynamical fingerprint: a crossover from long-lived prethermal memory to rapid thermalization, visible in single-atom-resolved densities.

</details>

### [118] [Hydrogen Molecular Ion and Molecule in Classical Electrodynamics with Classical Zero-Point Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22535)
*Timothy H. Boyer*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于包含经典零点辐射的经典电动力学，对氢分子离子（H₂⁺）和氢分子（H₂）进行了近似计算，发现经典理论中分子离子的结合能低于氢原子加远处质子，并给出了H₂⁺结合能2.1 eV、质子间距0.916 Å，H₂结合能4.6 eV、质子间距0.6 Å的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以在仅依赖经典电动力学（包括经典零点辐射）的框架下解释量子力学中已知的分子键合现象，从而检验经典理论对微观系统的解释能力。

Method: 采用包含经典零点辐射的经典电动力学近似计算方法，分别处理氢分子离子和氢分子的电子运动，考虑质子和原子之间的斥力作用以及电子对离子的静电吸引。

Result: 经典理论下，氢分子离子的结合能小于氢原子加远处质子的结合能，其结合能为2.1 eV，质子间距为0.916 Å；氢分子通过额外电子与离子的静电吸引形成，结合能为4.6 eV，质子间距为0.6 Å。

Conclusion: 经典电动力学结合零点辐射的方法能够近似描述氢分子离子和氢分子的键合特性，且离子结合较弱的原因可归结为质子和原子间在电子最自然共振轨道上的斥力作用。

Abstract: The hydrogen molecular ion and the hydrogen molecule are treated in an approximate calculation based on classical electrodynamics which includes classical zero-point radiation. It is found within the classical theory that a molecular ion is less-well bound than a hydrogen atom plus a distant proton. The smaller binding energy is explained due to the repulsive nature of the force between the proton and the atom when considering the most natural resonant orbit of the electron. The approximate classical electromagnetic calculation gives a binding energy of $2.1eV$ and a proton separation of $0.916A^{o}$ for the hydrogen molecular ion. The hydrogen molecule is formed by adding a single additional electron to the ion. The electrostatic attraction of the electron to the ion gives a binding energy of $4.6eV$ and a inter-proton separation of $0.6A^{o}$ for the hydrogen molecule in this classical electromagnetic approximation.

</details>


### [119] [The Photon Gas in Classical Mechanics: A Statistical-Mechanical Treatment of Classical Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23226)
*Farhang Loran,Saman Moghimi-Araghi*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究如何仅用经典电动力学推导热辐射热力学性质，证明能量密度、辐射压力与能量通量之间的正比关系无需借助光子或量子统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔体辐射热力学通常通过光子气体的量子理论引入，但作者希望探究这些热辐射特征中哪些可以仅从经典电动力学出发推导得出，从而厘清经典与量子框架各自的贡献与边界。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：第一种基于麦克斯韦方程组的显式解和电磁波的时空平均；第二种从第一性原理出发构建经典统计力学框架，包括构造正则哈密顿量、实现规范约束、将配分函数表述为泛函积分，并计算相应的场关联函数。

Result: 两种方法均证明能量密度、辐射压力和能量通量之间的正比关系可以在不引入光子和量子统计的情况下从经典电动力学中得出。

Conclusion: 通过将波动分析、统计力学、约束哈密顿动力学和泛函方法整合在单一经典框架内，该工作为电磁辐射热力学提供了一套自洽的经典表述，表明热辐射的若干基本特征本质上是经典电动力学的固有结论。

Abstract: The thermodynamics of cavity radiation is usually introduced through the quantum theory of a photon gas. In contrast, we investigate which features of thermal radiation follow from classical electrodynamics alone. We show, using two complementary approaches, that the proportionalities between energy density, radiation pressure, and energy flux arise without invoking photons or quantum statistics. The first approach is based on explicit solutions of Maxwell's equations and spatio-temporal averaging of electromagnetic waves. The second approach develops the classical statistical mechanics of the electromagnetic field from first principles by constructing the canonical Hamiltonian, implementing the gauge constraints, formulating the partition function as a functional integral, and evaluating the corresponding field correlation functions. By combining wave analysis, statistical mechanics, constrained Hamiltonian dynamics, and functional methods within a single classical framework, this work provides a coherent classical formulation of electromagnetic radiation thermodynamics.

</details>


<div id='nlin.PS'></div>

# nlin.PS [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Dynamics of Flat-Top--Bubble Vector Solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22405)
*M. O. D. Alotaibi,L. Al Sakkaf,U. Al Khawaja*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文通过变分近似和数值模拟研究了双组分平坦顶-气泡矢量孤子的动力学行为，揭示了局域平坦顶孤子通过自感应机制在第二组分中产生密度气泡并形成复合束缚态的物理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双组分平坦顶-气泡矢量孤子的动力学机制，特别是自感应气泡形成的物理过程及其稳定性特征，为理解耦合非线性系统中的复合束缚态提供理论依据。

Method: 采用变分近似方法，引入背景扣除公式和归一化超高斯分布，推导出组分中心和相对分离的有效相互作用势的集体坐标方程；同时结合实实时数值模拟验证变分预测。

Result: 变分近似预测了谐波内部振荡频率在小振荡区间内与振幅无关；实时模拟验证了弱踢行为的变分与数值频率具有良好一致性。对于强相位印记，通过将精确踢能量与变分束缚深度相等，预测了部分平坦顶孤子逃逸的临界踢尺度K_escape。数值模拟证实了这一预测：低于该尺度时复合体保持完整，高于该尺度时局域组分将大量离开气泡。

Conclusion: 研究表明，双组分平坦顶-气泡复合孤子的束缚势在整个位移范围内近似三角形、在最小值附近光滑且局部抛物线形，导致小振荡区间内振幅无关的谐波内部振荡；并给出了部分逃逸的临界条件，为操控此类复合孤子的动力学行为提供了定量预测手段。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of two-component flat-top--bubble vector solitons using a variational approximation and numerical simulations. The system is described by coupled cubic-quintic nonlinear Schrödinger equations with repulsive intercomponent coupling. We introduce a self-induced mechanism in which a localized flat-top soliton (FTS) generates a density bubble in a second component: the localized density acts as a repulsive potential for the background field, creating a composite bound state. A background-subtracted formulation and normalized super-Gaussian profiles yield collective-coordinate equations for the component centers and an effective interaction potential for their relative separation. The potential forms a binding well that is nearly triangular over its global displacement range but smooth and locally parabolic near its minimum. The variational approximation consequently predicts harmonic internal oscillations whose frequency is independent of amplitude in the small-oscillation regime. Real-time simulations verify this weak-kick behavior with good agreement between the variational and numerical frequencies. For strong phase imprints, equating the exact kick energy to the variational binding depth predicts a kick scale \(K_{\mathrm{escape}}\) for the onset of partial FTS escape. Real-time simulations support this prediction: below this scale, the composite remains nearly intact, whereas above it, a substantial fraction of the localized component leaves the bubble.

</details>


### [121] [Bifurcation structure and mesa pattern formation in a one-component nonlocal adhesion model with population pressure and degenerate mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22844)
*Shimpei Makida,Hideki Murakawa*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文分析了一类带种群压力与退化迁移率的一组分非局部黏附模型中的图案形成。通过线性稳定性分析推导了失稳阈值和最快增长模式的选取规则，解释了平均密度增加时选定波数向低波数移动的机制；通过弱非线性分析推导了Stuart–Landau方程中Landau系数的显式表达式，揭示了分岔的亚临界/超临界性质，并数值验证了大非线性指数促进亚临界转变及mesa图案的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非局部黏附模型在退化迁移率和种群压力作用下从均匀稳态产生图案形成的机制，特别是失稳条件、模式选择规则以及分岔性质，为生物聚集行为提供理论支撑。

Method: 采用线性稳定性分析推导失稳阈值和最快增长模式；通过弱非线性分析（Stuart–Landau方程）推导Landau系数；结合数值分岔分析与时间演化模拟验证理论结果；最后通过m趋于无穷的能量极限联系mesa剖面与容量约束极限结构。

Result: 得到了失稳阈值及最快增长模式，解释了波数随平均密度增加而降低的机制；推导出Landau系数的显式表达式，表明分岔性质（超临界/亚临界）取决于平均密度、非线性指数及核函数的二次谐波响应比；数值模拟证实了大非线性指数诱发亚临界转变、存在折叠点的分岔结构及mesa图案的形成。

Conclusion: 该模型展现了丰富的分岔行为：平均密度和非线性指数共同决定分岔的临界/亚临界性质，mesa图案的形成与容量约束极限结构相关联，为非局部聚集模型的图案动力学提供了完整的理论框架。

Abstract: We analyze pattern formation from a homogeneous steady state in a one-component nonlocal adhesion model with population pressure and degenerate mobility. First, using linear stability analysis, we derive the instability threshold and a selection rule for the fastest-growing mode, and elucidate the mechanism by which the selected wavenumber shifts toward lower wavenumbers as the mean density increases. We then perform a weakly nonlinear analysis near the critical adhesion strength and derive an explicit expression for the Landau coefficient in the Stuart--Landau equation. This expression shows that the critical bifurcation is classified as supercritical or subcritical according to the mean density, the nonlinear exponent, and the second-harmonic response ratio of the kernel. Furthermore, we show that a large nonlinear exponent promotes a transition to subcriticality and confirm, through numerical bifurcation analysis and time-dependent simulations, a bifurcation structure with a fold point and the formation of mesa patterns. Finally, through the energy limit as \(m\to\infty\), we relate the observed mesa profiles to a capacity-constrained limiting structure.

</details>
